00001         * PACMAIN : DRIVER ONLY. NO GAME RULES, NO I/O STATEMENTS.
00002         * PACPLAT (HOST)  PACINPT (CARDS)  PACGAME (RULES)  PACMAP (MAP)
00003         * PACAI (GHOSTS)  PACDISP (TEXT OUTPUT). SEE DOCS/ARCHITECTURE.MD
00004         * PARM 'TSO' [AIL N] REQUESTS THE INTERACTIVE TSO ADAPTER
00005         * (MVS-8): CONFIG COMES FROM THE PARM, THE BOARD IS SHOWN BEFORE
00006         * THE FIRST PROMPT. NO PARM = BATCH CARD MODE (REFERENCE).
00007         * NO INPUT IS READ ONCE THE GAME HAS ENDED (QUIT, WON, LOST).
00008         * OPTIONAL 'M' IN PARM COLUMN 11 (E.G. 'BAT       M' OR
00009         * 'TSO AI3 2 M') MAKES PACDISP PRINT A FINAL METRICS LINE.
00010         * COLUMN 12 = COLOUR (MVS-G1): A AUTO, Y YES, N/BLANK NO. PARM
00011         * 'TSD ...' IS THE BATCH DRY RUN OF THE COLOUR SCREENS (HEX).
00012          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00013          PROGRAM-ID. PACMAIN.
00014          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00015          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00016          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00017          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00018          DATA DIVISION.
00019          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00020          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00021 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00022 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00023 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00024 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00025 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00026 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00027 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00028 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00029 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00030 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00031 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00032 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00033 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00034 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00035 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00036 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00037 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00038 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00039 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00040 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00041 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00042 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00043 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00044 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00045 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00046 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00047 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00048          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00049 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00050 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00051 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00052 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00053 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00054 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.   2


00055 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00056 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00057 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00058 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00059 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00060 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00061 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00062 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00063 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00064          01  WS-CFG.
00065              05  WS-CFG-CH         PIC X OCCURS 5 TIMES.
00066          01  WS-K                  PIC 99.
00067          LINKAGE SECTION.
00068          01  PARM-AREA.
00069              05  PARM-LEN          PIC S9(4) COMP.
00070              05  PARM-MODE         PIC X(3).
00071              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00072              05  PARM-CFG          PIC X(5).
00073              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00074              05  PARM-MET          PIC X.
00075              05  PARM-COLOR        PIC X.
00076          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PARM-AREA.
00077          MAIN-PARA.
00078              MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00079              MOVE SPACE TO REQ-METRIC.
00080              IF PARM-LEN > 10 AND PARM-MET = 'M'
00081                  MOVE 'M' TO REQ-METRIC.
00082              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSO'
00083                  MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00084              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSD'
00085                  MOVE 'TSD' TO REQ-MODE.
00086              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-COLOR.
00087              IF PARM-LEN > 11
00088                  MOVE PARM-COLOR TO REQ-COLOR.
00089              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00090              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00091              MOVE 'RSET' TO REQ-OP.
00092              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00093              MOVE 'OPEN' TO REQ-OP.
00094              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00095              MOVE 'HEAD' TO REQ-OP.
00096              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00097              IF REQ-MODE = 'BAT'
00098                  PERFORM BAT-START THRU BAT-START-EXIT
00099              ELSE
00100                  PERFORM TSO-START THRU TSO-START-EXIT.
00101              PERFORM TURN-PARA THRU TURN-PARA-EXIT
00102                  UNTIL F-EOF = 1 OR F-QUIT = 1 OR F-WON = 1
00103                     OR F-LOST = 1.
00104              MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP.
00105              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00106              MOVE 'CLOS' TO REQ-OP.
00107              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00108              MOVE 'FINI' TO REQ-OP.
00109              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00110              STOP RUN.
00111          BAT-START.   3


00112              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00113              PERFORM CHECK-CONFIG THRU CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00114              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00115          BAT-START-EXIT.
00116              EXIT.
00117          TSO-START.
00118              IF PARM-LEN > 4
00119                  PERFORM COPY-PARM-CFG
00120                  MOVE 'PARM' TO REQ-OP
00121                  CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ
00122                  PERFORM APPLY-CONFIG THRU APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00123              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00124              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00125          TSO-START-EXIT.
00126              EXIT.
00127          COPY-PARM-CFG.
00128         * ONLY PARM-LEN BYTES ARE VALID: BLANK WHAT LIES BEYOND THEM.
00129              MOVE PARM-CFG TO WS-CFG.
00130              COMPUTE WS-K = PARM-LEN - 3.
00131              PERFORM BLANK-CFG-CH UNTIL WS-K > 5.
00132              MOVE WS-CFG TO REQ-TEXT.
00133          BLANK-CFG-CH.
00134              MOVE SPACE TO WS-CFG-CH (WS-K).
00135              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00136          SHOW-START.
00137              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00138              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00139              MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP.
00140              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00141          APPLY-CONFIG.
00142              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00143                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00144                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00145          APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00146              EXIT.
00147          READ-INPUT.
00148              MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP.
00149              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00150              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00151              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00152              IF REQ-KIND = 'EOF'
00153                  MOVE 'EOF ' TO REQ-OP
00154                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00155          READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00156              EXIT.
00157          CHECK-CONFIG.
00158              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00159                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00160                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00161                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00162          CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00163              EXIT.
00164          TURN-PARA.
00165              PERFORM DISPATCH THRU DISPATCH-EXIT.
00166              IF F-QUIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND F-LOST = 0
00167                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00168          TURN-PARA-EXIT.   4


00169              EXIT.
00170          DISPATCH.
00171              IF REQ-KIND = 'MOV'
00172                  MOVE 'MOVE' TO REQ-OP
00173                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00174                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00175                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00176              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'QUI'
00177                  MOVE 'QUIT' TO REQ-OP
00178                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00179              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'RDR'
00180                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00181                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00182              ELSE
00183                  MOVE 'INVL' TO REQ-OP
00184                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00185          DISPATCH-EXIT.
00186              EXIT.   5

00001         * PACGAME : GAME RULES AND STATE (PORTABLE, NO TERMINAL I/O).
00002         * OPS : RSET  DEFAULTS (3 LIVES, PLAYER 2,2, AI1, 1 GHOST)
00003         *       CFG   APPLY REQ-LEVEL / REQ-COUNT FROM THE CONFIG CARD

00062 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00063 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00064 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00065 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00066 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00067 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00068 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.

00075 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00076 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00077          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00078          MAIN-PARA.
00079              IF REQ-OP = 'RSET'
00080                  PERFORM DO-RESET
00081              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CFG '
00082                  PERFORM DO-CONFIG
00083              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00084                  PERFORM DO-INIT
00085              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'MOVE'
00086                  PERFORM DO-MOVE
00087              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'QUIT'
00088                  PERFORM DO-QUIT
00089              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'EOF '
00090                  MOVE 1 TO F-EOF.
00091              GOBACK.
00092          DO-RESET.
00093              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X.
00094              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y.
00095              MOVE 0 TO TICK-COUNT.
00096              MOVE 0 TO SCORE.
00097              MOVE 0 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00098              MOVE 3 TO LIVES.
00099              MOVE 'READY  ' TO GAME-STATE.
00100              MOVE ' ' TO LAST-CMD.
00101              MOVE 'START  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00102              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00103              MOVE 0 TO F-LOST.
00104              MOVE 0 TO F-QUIT.
00105              MOVE 0 TO F-EOF.
00106              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00107              MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.
00108              MOVE 1 TO GHOST-COUNT.
00109              MOVE 12345 TO GHOST-SEED.
00110          DO-CONFIG.
00111              MOVE REQ-LEVEL TO AI-LEVEL.   3


00112              MOVE REQ-COUNT TO GHOST-COUNT.
00113          DO-INIT.
00114              MOVE 'LOAD' TO M-OP.
00115              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00116              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00117              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00118              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00119              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00120              PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00121                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00122              PERFORM CLEAR-GHOST-CELL VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00123                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00124              MOVE 'CNT ' TO M-OP.
00125              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00126              MOVE M-COUNT TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00127          RESET-GHOST.
00128              MOVE GS-X (WS-G) TO GH-X (WS-G).
00129              MOVE GS-Y (WS-G) TO GH-Y (WS-G).
00130              MOVE GS-DIR (WS-G) TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00131          CLEAR-GHOST-CELL.
00132              MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO M-X.
00133              MOVE GH-Y (WS-G) TO M-Y.
00134              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00135              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00136          DO-QUIT.
00137              MOVE 1 TO F-QUIT.
00138              MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE.
00139          DO-MOVE.
00140              MOVE REQ-CHAR TO LAST-CMD.
00141              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00142              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00143              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00144              PERFORM AIM-MOVE THRU AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00145              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00146              MOVE NEW-X TO M-X.
00147              MOVE NEW-Y TO M-Y.
00148              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00149              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00150              IF M-FLAG = 1
00151                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO LAST-RESULT
00152              ELSE
00153                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00154                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00155                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00156              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00157              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00158          AIM-MOVE.
00159              IF REQ-DIR = 'U'
00160                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00161              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'D'
00162                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00163              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'L'
00164                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X
00165              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'R'
00166                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X.
00167          AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00168              EXIT.   4


00169          EAT-PELLET.
00170              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00171              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00172              MOVE 'EAT ' TO M-OP.
00173              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00174              IF M-FLAG = 1
00175                  ADD 10 TO SCORE
00176                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00177              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00178                  MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00179          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00180              EXIT.
00181          ROUND-END.
00182              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00183              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00184              IF F-HIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 0
00185                  CALL 'PACAI' USING PAC-STATE
00186                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00187              IF F-HIT = 1
00188                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00189          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00190              EXIT.
00191          CHECK-HIT.
00192              PERFORM HIT-TEST THRU HIT-TEST-EXIT
00193                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00194          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00195              EXIT.
00196          HIT-TEST.
00197              IF GH-X (WS-G) = PLAYER-X AND GH-Y (WS-G) = PLAYER-Y
00198                  MOVE 1 TO F-HIT.
00199          HIT-TEST-EXIT.
00200              EXIT.
00201          LOSE-LIFE.
00202              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00203              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00204              IF LIVES = 0
00205                  MOVE 1 TO F-LOST
00206                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00207              ELSE
00208                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00209                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00210                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00211                  PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00212                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00213                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00214                      MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00215          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00216              EXIT.
00217          SET-STATE.
00218              IF F-LOST = 1
00219                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00220              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00221                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE
00222              ELSE
00223                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00224          SET-STATE-EXIT.
00225              EXIT.   5

00058 C       * MAP SERVICE REQUEST: M-OP = LOAD WALL CLR  EAT  CNT
00059 C        01  MAP-REQ.
00060 C            05  M-OP                  PIC X(4).

00065          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00066          MAIN-PARA.
00067              MOVE 0 TO M-FLAG.
00068              IF M-OP = 'LOAD'
00069                  MOVE MAP-DATA TO MAP-AREA
00070              ELSE IF M-OP = 'WALL'
00071                  PERFORM DO-WALL THRU DO-WALL-EXIT
00072              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CLR '
00073                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00074              ELSE IF M-OP = 'EAT '
00075                  PERFORM DO-EAT THRU DO-EAT-EXIT
00076              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CNT '
00077                  PERFORM DO-COUNT.
00078              GOBACK.
00079          DO-WALL.
00080              IF M-X < 1 OR M-X > 20 OR M-Y < 1 OR M-Y > 10
00081                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG
00082              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '#'
00083                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00084          DO-WALL-EXIT.
00085              EXIT.
00086          DO-EAT.
00087              IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '.'
00088                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00089                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00090          DO-EAT-EXIT.
00091              EXIT.
00092          DO-COUNT.
00093              MOVE 0 TO M-COUNT.

00095                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00096          COUNT-ROW.
00097              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00098                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00099          COUNT-CELL.
00100              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00101                  ADD 1 TO M-COUNT.
00102          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00103              EXIT.   3

00077          MAIN-PARA.
00078              PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00079                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00080              GOBACK.
00081          GHOST-MOVE.
00082              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00083              MOVE GH-DIR (WS-G) TO WS-CUR.
00084              MOVE REV-D (WS-CUR) TO WS-REVD.
00085              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00086                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00087              IF CAND-N = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 1
00088                  PERFORM TRY-REVERSE THRU TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00089              IF CAND-N > 0
00090                  PERFORM CHOOSE-DIR THRU CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT
00091                  PERFORM STEP-GHOST.
00092          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00093              EXIT.
00094          GHOST-PROBE.
00095              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00096              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00097              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.
00098              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00099              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00100              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00101              IF M-FLAG = 0
00102                  IF AI-LEVEL = 1 OR WS-D NOT = WS-REVD
00103                      ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00104                      MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00105          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          TRY-REVERSE.
00108              MOVE WS-REVD TO WS-D.
00109              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00110              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00111              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.   3


00112              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00113              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00115              IF M-FLAG = 0
00116                  MOVE 1 TO CAND-N
00117                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (1).
00118          TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00119              EXIT.
00120          CHOOSE-DIR.
00121              IF AI-LEVEL = 3
00122                  PERFORM PICK-CHASE
00123              ELSE IF AI-LEVEL = 2 AND CAND-N = 1
00124                  MOVE CAND (1) TO WS-D
00125              ELSE
00126                  PERFORM PICK-RANDOM.
00127          CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT.
00128              EXIT.
00129          PICK-RANDOM.
00130              COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74.
00131              DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00132                  REMAINDER GHOST-SEED.
00133              DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00134                  REMAINDER WS-REM.
00135              ADD 1 TO WS-REM.
00136              MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D.
00137          PICK-CHASE.
00138              MOVE 999 TO BEST-DIST.
00139              PERFORM CHASE-EVAL THRU CHASE-EVAL-EXIT
00140                  VARYING WS-J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-J > CAND-N.
00141          CHASE-EVAL.
00142              MOVE CAND (WS-J) TO WS-K.
00143              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-K) - 1.
00144              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-K) - 1.
00145              COMPUTE DIST-X = G-NX - PLAYER-X.
00146              IF DIST-X < 0
00147                  COMPUTE DIST-X = 0 - DIST-X.
00148              COMPUTE DIST-Y = G-NY - PLAYER-Y.
00149              IF DIST-Y < 0
00150                  COMPUTE DIST-Y = 0 - DIST-Y.
00151              COMPUTE DIST-T = DIST-X + DIST-Y.
00152              IF DIST-T < BEST-DIST
00153                  MOVE DIST-T TO BEST-DIST
00154                  MOVE WS-K TO WS-D.
00155          CHASE-EVAL-EXIT.
00156              EXIT.
00157          STEP-GHOST.
00158              COMPUTE GH-X (WS-G) = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00159              COMPUTE GH-Y (WS-G) = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00160              MOVE WS-D TO GH-DIR (WS-G).   4

00001         * PACINPT : INPUT ADAPTER. ONE SYSIN CARD -> ONE REQUEST.
00002         * OPS : OPEN  READ  CLOS  PARM (CLASSIFY REQ-TEXT, NO I/O).
00003         * REQ-KIND: MOV (REQ-DIR U/D/L/R)  QUI  CFG (REQ-LEVEL,
00004         *   REQ-COUNT)  INV  EOF, AND IN TSO MODE RDR (BLANK LINE =
00005         *   REDRAW REQUEST). REQ-CHAR HOLDS THE COMMAND'S FIRST BYTE.
00006         * TSC (MVS-G1): READ ASKS PACPLAT RECV FOR ONE FULL-SCREEN INPUT;
00007         *   ENTER = THE TYPED FIELD, CLASSIFIED AS A CARD; ANY OTHER KEY
00008         *   (PA2 RESHOW) = RDR; AN ERROR LEAVES TSC AND READS A LINE.
00009         * ONLY THIS MODULE KNOWS ABOUT THE TERMINAL/CARD SOURCE.
00010          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00011          PROGRAM-ID. PACINPT.
00012          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00013          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00014          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00015          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00016          INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
00017          FILE-CONTROL.
00018              SELECT COMMAND-FILE ASSIGN TO UT-S-SYSIN.
00019          DATA DIVISION.
00020          FILE SECTION.
00021          FD  COMMAND-FILE
00022              RECORDING MODE IS F
00023              LABEL RECORDS ARE OMITTED
00024              RECORD CONTAINS 80 CHARACTERS
00025              DATA RECORD IS COMMAND-RECORD.
00026          01  COMMAND-RECORD        PIC X(80).
00027          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00028          01  WS-CARD.
00029              05  CMD-CHAR          PIC X.
00030              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00031              05  CMD-LEVEL         PIC X.
00032              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00033              05  CMD-COUNT         PIC X.
00034              05  FILLER            PIC X(75).
00035          01  RAW-CARD REDEFINES WS-CARD.
00036              05  RAW-CHAR          PIC X OCCURS 80 TIMES.
00037          01  NORM-REC.
00038              05  NR-CHAR           PIC X OCCURS 80 TIMES.
00039          01  WS-OFF                PIC 99.
00040          01  WS-I                  PIC 99.
00041          01  WS-J                  PIC 99.
00042          01  WS-LIM                PIC 99.
00043          LINKAGE SECTION.
00044          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00045 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00046 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00047 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00048 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00049 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00050 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00051 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00052 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00053 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00054 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).   2


00055 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00056 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00057 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00058 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00059 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00060          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ.
00061          MAIN-PARA.
00062              IF REQ-OP = 'OPEN'
00063                  OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE
00064              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSC'
00065                  PERFORM TSC-READ THRU TSC-READ-EXIT
00066              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ'
00067                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00068              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLOS'
00069                  CLOSE COMMAND-FILE
00070              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PARM'
00071                  MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD
00072                  MOVE REQ-TEXT TO WS-CARD
00073                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00074              GOBACK.
00075          DO-READ.
00076              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-KIND.
00077              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 'EOF' TO REQ-KIND.
00078              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00079                  MOVE COMMAND-RECORD TO WS-CARD.
00080              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'BAT'
00081                  PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00082              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'BAT'
00083                                   AND WS-CARD = SPACES
00084                  MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00085              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00086                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00087          DO-READ-EXIT.
00088              EXIT.
00089          TSC-READ.
00090              MOVE 'RECV' TO REQ-OP.
00091              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00092              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00093              IF REQ-INKEY = 'X'
00094                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00095                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00096              MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00097              IF REQ-INKEY NOT = 'E'
00098                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00099              MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD.
00100              MOVE REQ-INTEXT TO WS-CARD.
00101              PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00102              IF WS-CARD NOT = SPACES
00103                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00104          TSC-READ-EXIT.
00105              EXIT.
00106          NORMALIZE.
00107         * TSO/TSC ONLY: TOLERATE LEADING BLANKS/NON-PRINTABLE BYTES TYPED
00108         * BEFORE THE COMMAND: START AT THE FIRST BYTE > BLANK (1-8).
00109              MOVE 1 TO WS-OFF.
00110              PERFORM FIND-START UNTIL WS-OFF > 8
00111                  OR RAW-CHAR (WS-OFF) > SPACE.   3


00112              IF WS-OFF > 1 AND WS-OFF < 9
00113                  PERFORM SHIFT-LEFT THRU SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00114          NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00115              EXIT.
00116          FIND-START.
00117              ADD 1 TO WS-OFF.
00118          SHIFT-LEFT.
00119              MOVE SPACES TO NORM-REC.
00120              COMPUTE WS-LIM = 81 - WS-OFF.
00121              PERFORM COPY-CHAR VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1
00122                  UNTIL WS-I > WS-LIM.
00123              MOVE NORM-REC TO WS-CARD.
00124          SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00125              EXIT.
00126          COPY-CHAR.
00127              COMPUTE WS-J = WS-I + WS-OFF - 1.
00128              MOVE RAW-CHAR (WS-J) TO NR-CHAR (WS-I).
00129          CLASSIFY.
00130              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO REQ-CHAR.
00131              MOVE 'INV' TO REQ-KIND.
00132              IF CMD-CHAR = 'U' OR CMD-CHAR = 'u'
00133                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00134                  MOVE 'U' TO REQ-DIR
00135              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'D' OR CMD-CHAR = 'd'
00136                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00137                  MOVE 'D' TO REQ-DIR
00138              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'L' OR CMD-CHAR = 'l'
00139                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00140                  MOVE 'L' TO REQ-DIR
00141              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'R' OR CMD-CHAR = 'r'
00142                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00143                  MOVE 'R' TO REQ-DIR
00144              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'Q' OR CMD-CHAR = 'q'
00145                  MOVE 'QUI' TO REQ-KIND
00146              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'A'
00147                  MOVE 'CFG' TO REQ-KIND
00148                  PERFORM SET-CONFIG THRU SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00149          CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00150              EXIT.
00151          SET-CONFIG.
00152              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00153                  MOVE 0 TO REQ-LEVEL
00154              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '2'
00155                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-LEVEL
00156              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '3'
00157                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-LEVEL
00158              ELSE
00159                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-LEVEL.
00160              IF CMD-COUNT = '2'
00161                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-COUNT
00162              ELSE IF CMD-COUNT = '3'
00163                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-COUNT
00164              ELSE
00165                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-COUNT.
00166          SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00167              EXIT.   4

*OPTIONS IN EFFECT*     SIZE = 2097152  BUF = 1048576  LINECNT = 57  SPACE1, FLAGW,   SEQ,   SOURCE

00001         * PACDISP : DISPLAY ADAPTER (PLAIN TEXT, LEVEL G0). READS THE
00002         * STATE, NEVER CHANGES IT.  OPS : HEAD  STAT  INVL  END  PRMT
00003         * PRMT shows the input prompt in TSO mode only (no-op in BAT).
00004         * TSO: EVERY FRAME AFTER THE FIRST STARTS ON A FRESH SCREEN
00005         * (PACPLAT CLS), SO TSO NEVER PAGES A FRAME WITH '***'.
00006         * MVS-G0: COUNTS FRAMES, LINES, CHARACTERS AND SCREEN CLEARS;
00007         * THE TOTALS ARE PRINTED AFTER THE END LINES ONLY WHEN
00008         * REQ-METRIC = 'M' (OPTIONAL; NORMAL OUTPUT IS UNCHANGED).
00009         * MVS-G1: IN TSC (COLOUR FULL-SCREEN) AND TSD (ITS BATCH DRY RUN)
00010         * THE FRAME IS COMPOSED BY PACSCR AND SENT BY PACPLAT ONCE PER
00011         * READ (PRMT); NO LINE OUTPUT UNTIL THE END, WHEN PACPLAT FSOF
00012         * RESTORES LINE MODE BEFORE THE USUAL END LINES.
00013          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00014          PROGRAM-ID. PACDISP.
00015          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00016          CONFIGURATION SECTION.

00080          LINKAGE SECTION.

00106 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00107 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00108 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00109          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00110 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00111 C        01  PAC-REQ.   3


00112 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00113 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00114 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00115 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00116 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00117 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00118 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00119 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00120 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00121 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00122 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00123 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00124 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00125          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00126          MAIN-PARA.
00127              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSC' OR REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00128                  PERFORM SCREEN-OP THRU SCREEN-OP-EXIT
00129              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'HEAD'
00130                  DISPLAY 'PACMAN MVS-6 SMART GHOSTS'
00131                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00132                  ADD 25 TO M-CHARS
00133              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00134                  PERFORM SHOW-STATE THRU SHOW-STATE-EXIT
00135              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00136                  DISPLAY 'INVALID COMMAND ' REQ-CHAR
00137                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00138                  ADD 17 TO M-CHARS
00139              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00140                  PERFORM SHOW-END THRU SHOW-END-EXIT
00141              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00142                  PERFORM SHOW-PROMPT THRU SHOW-PROMPT-EXIT.
00143              GOBACK.
00144          SCREEN-OP.
00145              IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00146                  ADD 1 TO M-FRAMES
00147              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00148                  CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00149              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00150                  CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00151                  ADD DS-LEN TO M-CHARS
00152                  MOVE 'SEND' TO REQ-OP
00153                  CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00154                  MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP
00155              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00156                  MOVE 'FSOF' TO REQ-OP
00157                  CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ
00158                  MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP
00159                  PERFORM SHOW-END THRU SHOW-END-EXIT.
00160          SCREEN-OP-EXIT.
00161              EXIT.
00162          NEW-SCREEN.
00163              MOVE REQ-OP TO WS-SAVE-OP.
00164              MOVE 'CLS ' TO REQ-OP.
00165              ADD 1 TO M-CLEARS.
00166              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00167              MOVE WS-SAVE-OP TO REQ-OP.
00168          SHOW-PROMPT.   4


00169              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00170                  DISPLAY 'CMD (U/D/L/R/Q)?'
00171                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00172                  ADD 16 TO M-CHARS.
00173          SHOW-PROMPT-EXIT.
00174              EXIT.
00175          SHOW-END.
00176              IF F-LOST = 1
00177                  DISPLAY 'LOST'
00178                  ADD 4 TO M-CHARS
00179              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00180                  DISPLAY 'WIN'
00181                  ADD 3 TO M-CHARS
00182              ELSE IF F-QUIT = 1
00183                  DISPLAY 'QUIT'
00184                  ADD 4 TO M-CHARS
00185              ELSE
00186                  DISPLAY 'EOF'
00187                  ADD 3 TO M-CHARS.
00188              DISPLAY 'TURNS ' TICK-COUNT.
00189              DISPLAY 'SCORE ' SCORE.
00190              DISPLAY 'LIVES ' LIVES.
00191              ADD 4 TO M-LINES.
00192              ADD 26 TO M-CHARS.
00193              IF REQ-METRIC = 'M'
00194                  PERFORM SHOW-METRICS.
00195          SHOW-END-EXIT.
00196              EXIT.
00197          SHOW-METRICS.
00198              MOVE M-FRAMES TO MT-FRAMES.
00199              MOVE M-LINES TO MT-LINES.
00200              MOVE M-CHARS TO MT-CHARS.
00201              MOVE M-CLEARS TO MT-CLEARS.
00202              DISPLAY WS-MET.
00203          SHOW-STATE.
00204              ADD 1 TO M-FRAMES.
00205              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO' AND LAST-RESULT NOT = 'START  '
00206                  PERFORM NEW-SCREEN.
00207              MOVE TICK-COUNT TO ST-TURN.
00208              MOVE LAST-CMD TO ST-CMD.
00209              MOVE LAST-RESULT TO ST-RESULT.
00210              MOVE PLAYER-X TO ST-X.
00211              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO ST-Y.
00212              MOVE SCORE TO ST-SCORE.
00213              MOVE GH-X (1) TO ST-GX.
00214              MOVE GH-Y (1) TO ST-GY.
00215              MOVE LIVES TO ST-LIVES.
00216              MOVE GAME-STATE TO ST-STATE.
00217              MOVE PELLETS-LEFT TO ST-LEFT.
00218              DISPLAY WS-STATUS-LINE.
00219              ADD 1 TO M-LINES.
00220              ADD 61 TO M-CHARS.
00221              IF GHOST-COUNT > 1
00222                  PERFORM SHOW-EXTRA THRU SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00223              PERFORM SHOW-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00224                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00225          SHOW-STATE-EXIT.   5


00226              EXIT.
00227          SHOW-ROW.
00228              MOVE MAP-ROW (WS-ROW) TO WS-LINE.
00229              IF WS-ROW = PLAYER-Y
00230                  MOVE 'P' TO WS-LINE-CELL (PLAYER-X).
00231              PERFORM OVERLAY-GHOST THRU OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT
00232                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00233              DISPLAY WS-LINE.
00234              ADD 1 TO M-LINES.
00235              ADD 20 TO M-CHARS.
00236          OVERLAY-GHOST.
00237              IF WS-ROW = GH-Y (WS-G)
00238                  MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO WS-GX
00239                  MOVE 'G' TO WS-LINE-CELL (WS-GX).
00240          OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT.
00241              EXIT.
00242          SHOW-EXTRA.
00243              MOVE GH-X (2) TO XL2-X.
00244              MOVE GH-Y (2) TO XL2-Y.
00245              ADD 1 TO M-LINES.
00246              IF GHOST-COUNT = 2
00247                  DISPLAY WS-XL2
00248                  ADD 8 TO M-CHARS
00249              ELSE
00250                  MOVE GH-X (3) TO XL3-X
00251                  MOVE GH-Y (3) TO XL3-Y
00252                  DISPLAY WS-XL2 WS-XL3
00253                  ADD 17 TO M-CHARS.
00254          SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00255              EXIT.   6

00001         * PACPLAT : PLATFORM ADAPTER (MVS 3.8J). OPS : INIT FINI CLS
00002         * SEND RECV FSOF.  INIT validates REQ-MODE (BAT, TSO, TSD) and,
00003         * for TSO with REQ-COLOR A(UTO)/Y(ES), tries the TSC colour mode:
00004         * full-screen on (PACFSIO FSON); AUTO also needs QCode X'86' in
00005         * the Query Reply. Any failure leaves TSO line mode (monochrome
00006         * fallback). SEND/RECV move one TSC screen / one inbound; in TSD
00007         * (batch dry run) SEND prints the data stream in hex. An error in
00008         * TSC restores line mode and falls back to TSO. FSOF and FINI
00009         * always restore line mode if full-screen is active (PACFSIO
00010         * also restores it from an ESTAE exit on abend). CLS (TSO only)
00011         * starts a fresh line-mode screen via PACTSO. GAME/MAP/AI never
00012         * see any of this.
00013          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00014          PROGRAM-ID. PACPLAT.
00015          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00016          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00017          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00018          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00019          DATA DIVISION.
00020          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00021          01  TSO-OP                PIC X(4) VALUE 'CLS '.
00022          01  TSO-RC                PIC S9(4) COMP.
00023          01  FS-OP                 PIC X(4).
00024          01  FS-LEN                PIC S9(4) COMP.
00025          01  FS-RC                 PIC S9(4) COMP.
00026          01  FS-ACTIVE             PIC 9 VALUE 0.
00027          01  IN-AREA.
00028              05  IN-CH             PIC X OCCURS 2048 TIMES.
00029          01  HEXW.
00030              05  HX-Z              PIC X VALUE LOW-VALUE.
00031              05  HX-B              PIC X.
00032          01  HEXN REDEFINES HEXW   PIC 9(4) COMP.
00033          01  HEX-DATA              PIC X(16) VALUE '0123456789ABCDEF'.
00034          01  HEX-TABLE REDEFINES HEX-DATA.
00035              05  HEXDIG            PIC X OCCURS 16 TIMES.
00036          01  HEX-LINE.
00037              05  FILLER            PIC X(4) VALUE 'TSD '.
00038              05  HL-CH             PIC X OCCURS 64 TIMES.
00039          01  LEN-LINE.
00040              05  FILLER            PIC X(13) VALUE 'TSD FRAME LEN'.
00041              05  LL-LEN            PIC 9(4).
00042          01  WS-K                  PIC 9(4).
00043          01  WS-P                  PIC 9(4).
00044          01  WS-V                  PIC 9(4).
00045          01  WS-L                  PIC 9(4).
00046          01  WS-ID                 PIC 9(4).
00047          01  WS-QC                 PIC 9(4).
00048          01  WS-LIM                PIC 9(4).
00049          01  WS-O                  PIC 99.
00050          01  WS-Q                  PIC 99.
00051          01  WS-R                  PIC 99.
00052          01  WS-I                  PIC 99.
00053          01  WS-STOP               PIC 9.
00054          01  HAS-COLOUR            PIC 9.   2


00055          LINKAGE SECTION.
00056          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00057 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00058 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00059 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00060 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00061 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00062 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00063 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00064 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00065 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00066 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00067 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00068 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00069 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00070 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00071 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00072          01  PAC-DS COPY PACDS.
00073 C       * 3270 DATA STREAM BUFFER PASSED FROM PACSCR TO PACPLAT (SEND).
00074 C        01  PAC-DS.
00075 C            05  DS-LEN                PIC S9(4) COMP.
00076 C            05  DS-BUF.
00077 C                10  DS-BYTE           PIC X OCCURS 2048 TIMES.
00078          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00079          MAIN-PARA.
00080              IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00081                  PERFORM DO-INIT THRU DO-INIT-EXIT
00082              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FINI'
00083                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT
00084                  MOVE 0 TO RETURN-CODE
00085              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLS ' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00086                  CALL 'PACTSO' USING TSO-OP TSO-RC
00087              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'SEND'
00088                  PERFORM DO-SEND THRU DO-SEND-EXIT
00089              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'RECV'
00090                  PERFORM DO-RECV THRU DO-RECV-EXIT
00091              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FSOF'
00092                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00093              GOBACK.
00094          DO-INIT.
00095              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSO' AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSD'
00096                  MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00097              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00098                      AND (REQ-COLOR = 'A' OR REQ-COLOR = 'Y')
00099                  PERFORM TRY-COLOUR THRU TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00100          DO-INIT-EXIT.
00101              EXIT.
00102          TRY-COLOUR.
00103              MOVE 'FSON' TO FS-OP.
00104              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00105              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00106                  GO TO TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00107              MOVE 1 TO FS-ACTIVE.
00108              IF REQ-COLOR = 'Y'
00109                  MOVE 'TSC' TO REQ-MODE
00110                  GO TO TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00111              MOVE 'QRY ' TO FS-OP.   3


00112              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00113              MOVE 0 TO HAS-COLOUR.
00114              IF FS-RC = 0
00115                  PERFORM FIND-COLOUR THRU FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00116              IF HAS-COLOUR = 1
00117                  MOVE 'TSC' TO REQ-MODE
00118              ELSE
00119                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00120          TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00121              EXIT.
00122          FIND-COLOUR.
00123         * QUERY REPLY = AID X'88' (136) + STRUCTURED FIELDS: LENGTH(2),
00124         * X'81' (129), QCODE. COLOUR SUPPORT = QCODE X'86' (134).
00125              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00126              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00127              IF WS-V NOT = 136
00128                  GO TO FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00129              MOVE 2 TO WS-P.
00130              MOVE 0 TO WS-STOP.
00131              PERFORM NEXT-SF THRU NEXT-SF-EXIT UNTIL WS-STOP = 1.
00132          FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00133              EXIT.
00134          NEXT-SF.
00135              COMPUTE WS-LIM = WS-P + 3.
00136              IF WS-LIM > FS-LEN
00137                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP
00138                  GO TO NEXT-SF-EXIT.
00139              MOVE WS-P TO WS-K.
00140              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00141              COMPUTE WS-L = WS-V * 256.
00142              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00143              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00144              ADD WS-V TO WS-L.
00145              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00146              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00147              MOVE WS-V TO WS-ID.
00148              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00149              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00150              MOVE WS-V TO WS-QC.
00151              IF WS-ID = 129 AND WS-QC = 134
00152                  MOVE 1 TO HAS-COLOUR.
00153              IF WS-L < 4
00154                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP
00155              ELSE
00156                  ADD WS-L TO WS-P.
00157          NEXT-SF-EXIT.
00158              EXIT.
00159          DO-SEND.
00160              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00161                  PERFORM PRINT-STREAM THRU PRINT-STREAM-EXIT
00162                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00163              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC'
00164                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00165              MOVE 'SEND' TO FS-OP.
00166              MOVE DS-LEN TO FS-LEN.
00167              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP DS-BUF FS-LEN FS-RC.
00168              IF FS-RC NOT = 0   4


00169                  PERFORM FALL-BACK.
00170          DO-SEND-EXIT.
00171              EXIT.
00172          DO-RECV.
00173         * REQ-INKEY: E = ENTER (REQ-INTEXT = TYPED FIELD), P = ANY OTHER
00174         * AID (PA2 RESHOW ...), X = ERROR (NOW BACK IN TSO LINE MODE).
00175              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-INTEXT.
00176              MOVE 'X' TO REQ-INKEY.
00177              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC'
00178                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00179              MOVE 'RECV' TO FS-OP.
00180              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00181              IF FS-RC NOT = 0 OR FS-LEN < 1
00182                  PERFORM FALL-BACK
00183                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00184              MOVE 'P' TO REQ-INKEY.
00185              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00186              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00187              IF WS-V NOT = 125
00188                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00189              MOVE 'E' TO REQ-INKEY.
00190         * READ MODIFIED: AID, CURSOR(2), SBA X'11'(17) + ADDRESS(2), DATA.
00191              MOVE 4 TO WS-K.
00192              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00193              IF FS-LEN > 6 AND WS-V = 17
00194                  PERFORM COPY-TEXT THRU COPY-TEXT-EXIT
00195                      VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-I > 8.
00196          DO-RECV-EXIT.
00197              EXIT.
00198          COPY-TEXT.
00199              COMPUTE WS-K = WS-I + 6.
00200              IF WS-K NOT > FS-LEN
00201                  MOVE IN-CH (WS-K) TO REQ-INTEXT-CH (WS-I).
00202          COPY-TEXT-EXIT.
00203              EXIT.
00204          DO-FSOF.
00205              IF FS-ACTIVE = 1
00206                  MOVE 'FSOF' TO FS-OP
00207                  CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC
00208                  MOVE 0 TO FS-ACTIVE.
00209          DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00210              EXIT.
00211          FALL-BACK.
00212              PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00213              MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00214          BYTE-VAL.
00215              MOVE IN-CH (WS-K) TO HX-B.
00216              MOVE HEXN TO WS-V.
00217          PRINT-STREAM.
00218         * TSD DRY RUN: THE WHOLE DATA STREAM, 32 BYTES PER LINE IN HEX.
00219              MOVE DS-LEN TO LL-LEN.
00220              DISPLAY LEN-LINE.
00221              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00222              PERFORM PRINT-LINE UNTIL WS-K > DS-LEN.
00223          PRINT-STREAM-EXIT.
00224              EXIT.
00225          PRINT-LINE.   5


00226              MOVE SPACES TO HEX-LINE.
00227              MOVE 'TSD ' TO HEX-LINE.
00228              MOVE 0 TO WS-O.
00229              PERFORM PRINT-BYTE UNTIL WS-O > 62 OR WS-K > DS-LEN.
00230              DISPLAY HEX-LINE.
00231          PRINT-BYTE.
00232              MOVE DS-BYTE (WS-K) TO HX-B.
00233              DIVIDE HEXN BY 16 GIVING WS-Q REMAINDER WS-R.
00234              ADD 1 TO WS-Q.
00235              ADD 1 TO WS-R.
00236              ADD 1 TO WS-O.
00237              MOVE HEXDIG (WS-Q) TO HL-CH (WS-O).
00238              ADD 1 TO WS-O.
00239              MOVE HEXDIG (WS-R) TO HL-CH (WS-O).
00240              ADD 1 TO WS-K.   6
